# Repro: "RangeError: Maximum call stack exceeded" in `getTypeAtLocation` around 170 files in series with template literal strings

Reproduction case showing that calling the TypeScript `getTypeAtLocation` API to get types of the first file in a chain of about 170 that use template literal strings will generally give a `RangeError`.

Files are set up in a singly linked list of `export`s and `import`s.
Within each case:

- `src/index.ts` imports a `pipe0` function from `src/pipe0.ts`
- `src/pipe0.ts` imports a `pipe1` function from `src/pipe1.ts`
- ...
- The last pipe file `N` exports a `pipe${N}` function

Each `pipe*` function wraps a parameter inside a template literal expression.

[typescript-eslint's typed linting](https://typescript-eslint.io/getting-started/typed-linting) is then run on the `src/` directory.

## Setup

Use [Yarn](https://yarnpkg.com) and run the `comparison` script to execute cases locally:

```shell
yarn
yarn comparison
```

## Results

Measurements taken on:

- Hardware: M1 Max Mac Studio
- Platform: Node 22

Results are nondeterministic and generally look something like...

## Testing Notes

- Tested on TypeScript `4.7.3`, `5.1.3`, and `5.3.3`: all reproduce the issue.
- Avoiding template literal strings fixes the errors.
  Run `NON_TEMPLATE_STRINGS=true yarn comparison` to reproduce this.
